{"forms":[{"form":"Klein paradoxes","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["A surprising result of applying the Dirac equation to the problem of electron scattering from a potential barrier. {"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"more subject","tags":["comparative"]},{"form":"most subject","tags":["superlative"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"adj","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"a country subject to extreme heat","type":"example"},{"text":"Menu listings and prices are subject to change.","type":"example"},{"text":"He's subject to sneezing fits.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Likely to be affected by or to experience something; liable."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The local board sets local policy, subject to approval from the State Board.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Conditional upon something; used with to."]},{"glosses":["Placed or situated under; lying below, or in a lower situation."]},{"glosses":["Placed under the power of another; owing allegiance to a particular sovereign or state."]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"sŭbʹjĕkt"},{"ipa":"/ˈsʌb.d͡ʒɛkt/"},{"ipa":"/ˈsʌb.d͡ʒɪkt/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]}],"upstream_line":16792,"upstream_offset":113320500,"upstream_record_sha256":"8c99175e25f782d7c876fa75bf7d899d916a226f0f201ef3be0ef6cd97ebab50","word":"subject"}
{"etymology_number":"2","forms":[{"form":"subjects","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"antonyms":[{"word":"object"},{"word":"predicate"}],"examples":[{"text":"In the active sentence ‘The cat ate the mouse’, ‘the cat’ is the subject and ‘the mouse’ is the object.","type":"example"},{"text":"In the passive sentence ‘the mouse was eaten by the cat’, ‘the mouse’ is the subject even though it is the target (patient) of the action, and ‘the cat’ is the agent.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The noun, pronoun or noun phrase about whom the statement is made. In active clauses with verbs denoting an action, the subject is the actor. In clauses in the passive voice the subject is the target of the action."],"topics":["grammar","human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"the subjects and objects of power","type":"example"}],"glosses":["By extension: an actor or agent; one who takes action."]},{"glosses":["The main topic of a paper, work of art, discussion, field of study, etc."]},{"examples":[{"text":"Her favorite subject is physics.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A particular area of study."]},{"examples":[{"text":"I am a British subject.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A citizen in a monarchy."]},{"glosses":["A person ruled over by another, especially a monarch or state authority."]},{"glosses":["The main theme or melody, especially in a fugue."],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]},{"examples":[{"text":"human subject research","type":"example"},{"text":"animal subjects","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A human, animal, or an inanimate object that is being examined, treated, analysed, etc; especially, one being studied in a scientific experiment, such as a clinical trial."]},{"glosses":["A being that has subjective experiences, subjective consciousness, or a relationship with another entity."],"topics":["human-sciences","philosophy","sciences"]},{"glosses":["That of which something is stated."],"topics":["human-sciences","logic","mathematics","philosophy","sciences"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Making x the subject of x²-6x#43;3y#61;0, we get x#61;3#92;pm#92;sqrt#123;9-3y#125;.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A variable isolated on one side of an equality, expressed in terms of other variables."],"topics":["mathematics","sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"enpr":"sŭbʹjĕkt"},{"ipa":"/ˈsʌb.d͡ʒɛkt/"},{"ipa":"/ˈsʌb.d͡ʒɪkt/","tags":["Received-Pronunciation"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"discussion","word":"matter"},{"sense":"discussion","word":"topic"}],"upstream_line":16793,"upstream_offset":113334506,"upstream_record_sha256":"22f1eddc1ca14098d59e3a055d3006c0cd2eedd7e7b5ebe1eb1777fe95b6d955","word":"subject"}
